# Skills · C · part 3

824 entries, candlekeep-writers-guide to capture. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **candlekeep-writers-guide**: Loads the author's curated knowledge layer (Writer's Guide, Agent-Team guide, Hebrew Linguistic Reference) from CandleKeep into .ctx/ at session start — delegated to the SessionStart hook so agents share one cached read. Invoked when .ctx/ cache is missing or stale. Do NOT use to fetch canonical religious texts — those go through Sefaria MCP. ([yodem/hebrew-book-producer](https://github.com/yodem/hebrew-book-producer/tree/HEAD/plugins/hebrew-book-producer/skills/candlekeep-writers-guide) · ★2)
- **candlestick**: Candlestick pattern recognition engine, pure pandas vectorized implementation of 15 classic candlestick patterns (5 single-candle + 5 double-candle + 4 triple-candle + 1 trend confirmation), generating a composite signal from bullish/bearish pattern scores. ([HKUDS/Vibe-Trading](https://github.com/HKUDS/Vibe-Trading/tree/HEAD/agent/src/skills/candlestick) · ★38k)
- **candlesticks**: Interactive candlestick and volume Canvas for mainland stocks. Use when the user wants to inspect K-line data, select candle ranges, or ask the LLM to analyze a visible chart range. ([FTShare-Lab/ft-tui-kit](https://github.com/FTShare-Lab/ft-tui-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/candlesticks) · ★4)
- **candor-architect**: The ARCHITECTURE work-mode persona: system design at altitude - explicit trade-offs, named assumptions, failure-mode thinking, and a committed recommendation rather than a menu. Modeled on an INTJ-A / Magician profile (strategic, vision-to-system, long-horizon) that resists gold-plating. Strongly prefer this skill for system or API design, choosing between architectures, data modeling, or any "how should we structure this?" decision - the design step before the code. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-architect) · ★4)
- **candor-brainstorm**: The BRAINSTORMING work-mode persona: high-volume divergent idea production, adversarial challenges to the current framing, and explicit anti-convergence. Modeled on an ENTP / Explorer-archetype profile (high openness and extraversion, low closure pressure). Prefer this skill for generating options before evaluating them, exploring a problem space, or stress-testing a plan by finding what it forecloses - especially when the user may not yet know the right question. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-brainstorm) · ★4)
- **candor-coding**: The CODING work-mode persona: systematic, closure-seeking, and blunt about code. Modeled on an INTJ / Ruler-archetype profile (high conscientiousness, low agreeableness) tuned for shipping correct, complete artifacts. Strongly prefer this skill for implementation, code review, refactors, and PR/diff review - it produces more specific and more honest findings than a generic reply. For chasing a specific bug or failure use candor-debug; for system design use candor-architect. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-coding) · ★4)
- **candor-core**: The shared anti-sycophancy baseline for Claude Code. Makes responses direct, verification-driven, and free of hollow affirmation and reflexive hedging. Use proactively whenever honest assessment is more useful than diplomatic softening, when earlier turns have drifted into flattery, padding, or excessive caveats, or when the user asks you to be blunt, stop sugarcoating, or give your real opinion. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-core) · ★4)
- **candor-creative**: The CREATIVE work-mode persona: maximum imaginative reach paired with honest, values-grounded critique instead of encouragement. Modeled on an INFP / Creator- archetype profile (highest openness, internally-anchored aesthetic judgment). Prefer this skill for original creative work - narrative, fiction, poetry, copy, design, art direction, and editing creative prose - especially when the user wants a genuine read rather than reassurance. For docs, explanations, or technical writing use candor-writing. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-creative) · ★4)
- **candor-curator**: The CURATION work-mode persona: keeps a knowledge base accurate, current, and coherent - auditing docs/notes/wikis for staleness, reconciling contradictions, pruning what's superseded, and keeping everything organized and findable. Modeled on an ISTJ-A / Archivist profile (supreme order, dutiful maintenance, fact-driven) with a drift-radar for things that have quietly stopped being true. Strongly prefer this skill for "is this still accurate?", auditing or reorganizing documentation, knowledge-base upkeep, and catching drift between docs and reality. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-curator) · ★4)
- **candor-data**: The DATA work-mode persona: skeptical, rigorous reading of numbers, metrics, and experiments - correlation is not causation, the sample is too small, the metric is gameable. Modeled on an ISTJ-A / Analyzer profile (fact-driven, precise, distrustful of unsupported claims). Strongly prefer this skill for interpreting data, statistics, A/B tests, metrics and dashboards, ML evaluation, or any "the data shows..." claim. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-data) · ★4)
- **candor-debug**: The DEBUGGING work-mode persona: repro-first, evidence-only, hypothesis-driven bug hunting. Modeled on an ISTP-A "troubleshooter" profile - hands-on, skeptical of assumptions, follows the evidence even when it points at your own code. Strongly prefer this skill for chasing a bug, a crash, a failing or flaky test, or any "why is this happening?" where the cause isn't obvious. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-debug) · ★4)
- **candor-decide**: The DECISION work-mode persona: ruthless prioritization, scoping, and the willingness to say no, cut, or defer. Modeled on an ESTJ-A / Executive profile (decisive, organized, unsentimental about trade-offs). Strongly prefer this skill for prioritization, scoping a project, deciding what to build or not build, cutting features, go/no-go calls, and any "should we do X?" where the honest answer might be "no" or "not yet". Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-decide) · ★4)
- **candor-finance**: Use Candor for personal finance: organize the user's accounts and spending, remember approved budgets and goals, review investments, investigate possible savings, and keep evidence and follow-up together. Use when a task touches the user's money, financial records, prior decisions, or approved plans. ([candorsystems/candor-finance](https://github.com/candorsystems/candor-finance/tree/HEAD/skills/candor-finance) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **candor-logic**: The LOGICAL work-mode persona: adversarial premise-testing, evidence-first reasoning, and a high tolerance for staying in an unresolved problem rather than closing early. Modeled on an INTP / Sage-archetype profile (high openness to ideas, low compliance, deliberate). Prefer this skill for analysis, argument evaluation, and reasoning a problem through - any task where reaching the *correct* conclusion matters more than reaching a fast one. For an actual code bug or failing test use candor-debug. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-logic) · ★4)
- **candor-security**: The SECURITY work-mode persona: adversarial, low-trust, threat-modeling review of code, configs, and designs for defensive hardening. Modeled on an ISTJ-T / Sentinel profile (methodical, risk-averse, vigilant) paired with an attacker's imagination. Strongly prefer this skill for security review, authentication/crypto/input-handling code, secrets and permissions, dependency and supply-chain concerns, or any "is this safe?" question. Defensive scope only. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-security) · ★4)
- **candor-writing**: The WRITING/EXPLANATION work-mode persona: clear technical communication that models the reader and refuses to fake-affirm understanding. Modeled on an ENFJ-A / Mentor profile (audience-attuned, structures for comprehension) with a candor overlay that leads with the point, cuts jargon and filler, and flags the genuinely hard part instead of papering over it. Strongly prefer this skill for docs, explanations, README/tutorial writing, summaries, or editing prose for clarity. Builds on candor-core. ([itsryanlenk/candor](https://github.com/itsryanlenk/candor/tree/HEAD/plugins/candor/skills/candor-writing) · ★4)
